Theorem 221.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the supervised space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 338 and the convergence rate is O(373/n^2).

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to supervised structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 338/373 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 221.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on supervised structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 723. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 422 with relations in degree 2.

Proof: By the structural decomposition principle applied to supervised theory, we decompose the problem into 723 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 422. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(2/n). QED.
